```
def make_conv_matrix_dense(f):
    n=f.size
    A = np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for d, val in enumerate(f):
        if val !=0:
            A[d:d+n-d, :n-d] = val
    return A
```
This fills all diagonal segments. Complexity ∑ (n-d) ~ n(n+1)/2. For 800 320k, fine. For n=1024 523k, fine. For n=2048 2M, fine. Python loop 2048, each vector assignment; okay maybe 0.1s. Could optimize usingbroadcasting? Maybe not.

Now let's design `local_search` in detail.

Function `local_search(a, budget_s, seed=0, use_lp=False, lp_budget=0.0, label="")`:
Input a length n. Returns best, best_score.
Steps:
- Normalize target S=sqrt(2n). Use target S not n. `S = math.sqrt(2.0*n)`.
- c, score.
- best=a
- t0, deadline.
- Choose lp_phase if use_lp and n<=1024 and enough time.
Maybe structure:
```
while time < deadline:
   # switch between LP and gradient intervals.
   if use_lp and n<=1024:
       if time left >60: try lp_improve for min(remaining, 120) (multiple LP iters internally)
   # then gradient search for maybe 60s
```
But simpler: within local_search, first call `lp_refine(a, lp_budget, ...)` if budget_s>100 and n<=1024. Then call `gradient_refine` with remaining. But after gradient, another LP might help. Use loop:
```
phase = 'lp' if use_lp ... else 'grad'
for _ in range(2):
   if phase=='lp'...
   else...
```
But if one method gets stuck, need restarts. We can include restarts in gradient_refine: if stagnant > threshold, smooth_perturb best.

`lp_refine(a, budget_s, seed)`:
- `a = normalize`
- best, score.
- deadline.
- Iteration count.
- For it in range(max_lp_iters): if time < deadline - 5 break.
   c, M. if M < best_score? update?
   g = solve_lp_direction(a, M)
   if g None break
   # line search over t samples
   improved = False
   for t in sample_ts:
       h=normalize((1-t)*a + t*g,S)
       sc=fast_score(h)
       if sc<best_score*(1-1e-12): best=h; best_score=sc; improved=True
   # also accept best h for next even if not global? Choose h with min score among maybe not improving.
   # Next a = best_h (if score <= current) or maybe 0.1 interpolation.
   if not improved: break
   a=best
- return best, score.

Compute q via FFT. Efficient `corr_valid(a,w)`:
```
def cross_valid(a, w):
    n = a.size
    L = 2*n-1
    N = 1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length() # >=3n-2
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N) # since conv with reverse w
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,N)[:3*n-2]
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Need verify sign. Earlier q_j=Σ_d a[d] w[d+j] from conv(a, w[::-1]) with q=conv[n-1:2n-1][::-1]. w length 2n-1. Conv length 3n-2. Test small n=2, a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. `w_rev=[w2,w1,w0]`. conv: 0:a0 w2; 1:a0w1+a1w2; 2:a0w0+a1w1; 3:a1w0. q0=a0w0+a1w1 index2; q1=a0w1+a1w2 index1. For n=2, slice [n-1:2*n-1] = [1:3] -> indices1,2; reverse -> [2,1] = q0,q1. Good. Work. For general, yes. Use `rfft` length N≥3n-2. Complexity 2 forward rfft plus inverse. `local_search`:
```
def local_search(a, budget_s, seed, use_lp=False):
    a=normalize(...)
    n=a.size; S=math.sqrt(2*n)
    deadline = now+budget
    best, best_score = a, fast_score(a)
    # loop until deadline
    it=0; stagnant=0; lr=0.1; temp_log=0.4
    mom=np.zeros(n)
    while time < deadline-5:
        # maybe do LP if use_lp and n<=1024 and every X
        do_lp = use_lp and n<=1024 and (it < 20 or stagnant<...? ) and time_left>lp_cost
        # To avoid repeating LP too often, use if it % lp_interval ==0 or at start.
        if do_lp and time_left > 30:
            new = lp_step(a,m) ... # one LP line search iteration
            if new_score < best_score: update a,best; stagnant=0; it+=1; continue
            # else continue gradient? maybe no improvement
        # gradient step
        c = conv(a); score=fast_score
        if score < best_score update
        p=weights_log(c)
        grad=gradient_log(a,c,p,S)
        # if grad scale zero: break
        mom = 0.5*mom +0.5*grad
        delta = -lr*mom
        delta=clip(delta,-3,3)
        cand = a * exp(delta + noise)
        cand=normalize(cand,S)
        cand_score=fast_score(cand)
        if cand_score < best_score*(1-1e-12): best=cand; best_score=...
        if cand_score <= score*(1+accept_tol): accept a=cand; score=cand_score; relax lr/temp; stagnant=0? Actually stagnant if best not improve.
        else: reject, shrink lr, stagnant++
        if stagnant > 300:
            a=smooth_perturb(best); best? keep; reset lr/temp
    return best,best_score
```

Let's refine `weights_log_objective`.
Option implementation:
```
def weights_log_objective(c, temp=0.3, top_frac=0.005):
    L=c.size
    p=np.zeros(L)
    max_c = float(c.max())
    if max_c <=0: p[0]=1; return p
    pos = c > max(1e-12*max_c, 1e-300)
    if not pos.any(): p[np.argmax(c)]=1; return p
    logc = np.zeros(L) # no need full log
    logc_pos = np.log(c[pos])
    max_log = logc_pos.max()
    w = np.zeros(L)
    w[pos] = np.exp((logc_pos - max_log)/temp)
    ws = w.sum()
    if ws>0: p = w/ws
    else: p[argmax]=1
    # mix top uniform
    K = max(1, int(L*top_frac))
    if K < L:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        q = np.zeros(L); q[idx]=1/len(idx)
        p = 0.7*p + 0.3*q  # weights near max
        p /= p.sum()
    return p
```
If K=L (small), no top. Top fraction 0.01 means 20 for n=1024, 40 for 2048. Good. `temp` in log units: 0.3 means c within factor e^{0.3}=1.35. Top mix ensures at least top1%. For objective max, maybe only top few. Good.

`gradient_log`:
```
def gradient_log(a,c,p,target_sum):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    pos = c > 0
    w=np.zeros(L)
    w[pos] = 2.0 * p[pos] / c[pos]
    q = cross_valid(a,w)
    grad = a * q
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S > 0:
       grad = grad - 2.0*a/S
    # center small
    grad -= np.mean(grad)
    return grad
```
Check if p top uniform but c variations: for top not all same, w=p/c. Sum grad =2? Sum a q =2Σp_k (c_k/c_k) if q exact, but top p with p sum1 over top K, yes 2 regardless. Then -2a/S sum -2. Good. If p has top and log mixture sum1, good. Numerical center.

But if c_k=0 p maybe zero; fine. `cross_valid` with w negative? w positive. Good.

def lp_refine(a, budget_s, seed=0):
    if budget_s < 20: return a, fast_score
    n=a.size; target=S=sqrt(2n); a=normalize; deadline
    best=a; best_score=fast_score
    it=0
    while time < deadline - 20:
        c=fast_convolution(a); score=fast_score(a)
        if scored? update best
        M=max(c)*(1+1e-7)+1e-12
        p=weights_log_objective(c, 0.25)
        # Objective bias d = cross_valid(a, 2*p? no /c?) For derivative wrt g of F (max c) active p: d_i=2Σ p_k a[k-i]. w=2p. d=cross_valid(a, w).
        w=2*p
        d=cross_valid(a,w)
        dmax=max(d); if dmax>0: d_norm=d/dmax
        g = solve_lp_direction(a, M, d_norm, n)
        if g None: break
        # line search 21 t
        best_mix=a; best_mix_score=best_score
        for t in [0.01, 0.02,...,1]:
             h=(1-t)*a + t*g
             h=np.maximum(h,0); h=normalize(h,target)
             sc=fast_score(h)
             if sc < best_mix_score: ...
             if sc < best_score: best=h; best_score=sc; best_mix=h; best_mix_score=sc
        if best_mix_score < score*(1-1e-12): a=best_mix; it?; continue
        else: break
        it++
        if it > max_lp_iters (e.g. 50) break
    return best,best_score
```

def smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); n=a.size; target=math.sqrt(2*n)
    if n>2:
       t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       z=0
       for m in range(1,5):
          amp=strength/(0.5+0.5*m)
          ph=rng.uniform(0,2pi)
          z += amp*np.cos(2pi*m*t+ph)
       a *= np.exp(z)
    if n>4:
       width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//10+1)))
       start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
       a[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,strength,width))
    a=np.maximum(a, floor)
    a=normalize_sequence(a,target)
    return a
```

Set global `_LBFGS_TEMP = 0.3`. Top-level:
```
_LB_TEMP = 0.3

def _lbfgs_x_to_b(x):
   b = np.asarray(x,float)
   return b - b.mean()

def _lbfgs_objective(x):
   b = x - mean
   a = np.exp(b)
   c = fast_convolution(a)
   logc = np.log(np.maximum(c, 1e-300))
   max_log = logc.max()
   exps = np.exp((logc-max_log)/_LB_TEMP)
   logsum = _LB_TEMP*(max_log + math.log(exps.sum()))
   S = a.sum()
   return float(logsum - 2*math.log(S) + math.log(2*b.size))

def _lbfgs_gradient(x):
   b=...
   a=exp(b)
   c=...
   logc=...
   max_log=...
   p=exp((logc-max_log)/_LB_TEMP); p/=sum
   # gradient log max approximation: G = a * cross_valid(a, 2*p/c) - 2*a/S
   invw = 2*p/c
   q=cross_valid(a, invw)
   grad = a*q
   S=a.sum(); grad -= 2*a/S
   grad -= mean(grad)
   return grad
```

Now, global L-BFGS functions need access to temp and maybe max log safe. Let's implement:
```
_LB_TEMP = 0.3
_LB_N = 0

def _lbfgs_make_b(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0: return x
    return x - float(np.mean(x))

def _lbfgs_obj_val(x):
    b = _lbfgs_make_b(x)
    n=b.size
    a = np.exp(b)
    c = fast_convolution(a)
    max_c = float(c.max())
    if max_c <=0: return 0.0
    logc_max = math.log(max_c)
    # stable over positive c; if some c zero? a positive => c positive
    pos = c > 0
    if not pos.all(): 
       # handle zeros: logc where pos, -inf zeros. Use max_log, exp((logc-max)/T); zeros ignored.
    logc = np.full(c.size, -np.inf)
    logc[pos] = np.log(c[pos])
    exps = np.zeros(c.size)
    exps[pos] = np.exp((logc[pos]-logc_max)/_LB_TEMP)
    logsum = _LB_TEMP*(logc_max + math.log(max(exps.sum(), 1e-300)))
    S = float(a.sum())
    return float(logsum - 2.0*math.log(S) + math.log(2.0*n))
```
If some c zero, logc_max still; exps sum >0. OK.

Gradient:
```
def _lbfgs_grad(x):
    b=_lbfgs_make_b(x); n=b.size; a=np.exp(b)
    c=fast_convolution(a); S=a.sum()
    max_c=float(c.max())
    if max_c<=0: return zeros
    logc_max=math.log(max_c)
    pos=c>0
    exps=np.zeros(c.size); exps[pos]=exp((log(c[pos])-logc_max)/T)
    p=exps/exps.sum()
    w=np.zeros(c.size); w[pos]=2.0*p[pos]/c[pos]
    q=cross_valid(a,w)
    grad=a*q - 2*a/S
    grad -= grad.mean()
    return grad
```
Note if objective uses T logsumexp(logc/T), exact gradient p as above. For `-2logS` coefficient is -2, yes. Constant log(2n) no gradient.

def lbfgs_refine(a, budget_s, seed=0):
    # a positive
    n=a.size
    x0 = np.log(np.maximum(a, tiny))
    x0 -= x0.mean()
    best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
    T_list = [0.8,0.4,0.2,0.1]
    for T in T_list:
       if time > deadline break
       global _LB_TEMP
       _LB_TEMP = float(T)
       bounds = [(-6.0,6.0)]*n
       # maxiter based on n and budget
       if n <=512: maxiter=400
       elif n<=1024: maxiter=200
       else: maxiter=80
       try:
          res = scipy.optimize.minimize(_lbfgs_objective, x0, jac=_lbfgs_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'iprint': 0, 'gtol': 1e-5, 'maxls': 20})
          if res.x finite:
             b=res.x - mean(res.x); a2=np.exp(b); a2=normalize; sc=fast_score
             if sc<best_score: best=a2; best_score=sc; x0=res.x? maybe update x0 = res.x (or mean0)
          else: a2=... from x0
       except Exception: pass
    # final polish with custom gradient
    return best, score
```

`fast_convolution`:
```
def fast_convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.empty(0)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    L=2*n-1
    N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N, n=N)
    c=c[:L].copy()
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c
```
`irfft` parameter `n=N`.

`fast_score_array`:
```
def fast_score_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0); a=np.maximum(a,0.0); a=np.minimum(a,1000.0?)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    c=fast_convolution(a)
    maxc=float(c.max())
    if maxc<=0: return inf? If all zero sum? If sum>0 but c zero impossible.
    return float(2.0*n*maxc/(s*s))

`make_harmonic`: Use t and rng. Return positive.
```
def make_harmonic_sequence(n,rng):
   if n<=1: return np.ones(n)
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,7): amp=rng.normal(0,1)/m; ph=rng.uniform(0,2pi); z+=amp*np.cos(2pi*m*t+ph)
   z -= z.max()
   return np.exp(z)
```

def make_lp_matrix(a):
   n=a.size; m=2*n-1; A=np.zeros((m,n)); idx=np.arange(n)
   for d in range(n):
      val=float(a[d])
      if val != 0.0:
         A[d+d? no d+idx, idx] = val
   return A
```
For d from0 to n-1, rows d:d+n. But A has m rows; d+n <=2n-1? d=n-1 -> d+n=2n-1, exclusive end ok. Columns 0:n. Good.

`solve_lp_direction(a, M, bias_vec, target_sum, time_limit=20.0)`:
```
n=a.size
A=make_lp_matrix(a)
if bias_vec is not None and bias_vec.size==n and max>0:
   bmax=float(np.max(bias_vec)); bvec=np.maximum(bias_vec,0)
   if bmax>1e-14: bias =0.5 * (bvec/bmax)
   c_obj = -1.0 + bias
else: c_obj = -np.ones(n)
b_ub=M*np.full(2*n-1,1.0)
bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n
opts = {'presolve': True, 'time_limit': time_limit, 'maxiter': 20000}
try: res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=opts)
except Exception:
   try: res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
   except Exception: return None
x=getattr(res,'x',None)
if x is None: return None
x=np.asarray(x,float); x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0); x=np.maximum(x,0)
s=float(x.sum())
if s <= 1e-12: return None
g=x*target_sum/s
# ensure f*g <= M, scale down if violation (use fast_convolution)
fg=fast_convolution(a,g)
maxfg=float(fg.max()) if fg.size else 0
tol=M*1e-6+1e-12
if maxfg > M and maxfg>tol:
    g *= M/maxfg
return g
```

def lp_refine(a,budget_s,seed=0):
   n=a.size; target=math.sqrt(2*n); a=normalize_sequence(a,target); deadline=...
   best=a; best_score=fast_score(a)
   tlist = [0.01,0.02,...]

       c=fast_convolution(a); score=fast_score(a)
       if score<best_score: best...
       M=max(c)*(1+1e-7)+1e-12

       d=cross_valid(a,p)

       if g is None: break
       # line search

             best_mix_score=min(...); best_mix=h
       # need track local min
```

`weights_log_objective`: Need top partition. For n large, argpartition each call in LP only few times; in gradient many. For gradient, use temp only maybe no top partition for speed. Let's implement with optional `use_top` boolean.
```
def weights_log_objective(c, temp=0.25, top_frac=0.0):
    L=c.size
    p=np.zeros(L)
    maxc=float(c.max())
    if maxc<=0: p[0]=1; return p
    pos=c>max(1e-12*maxc, 1e-300)
    if not np.any(pos): p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; return p
    logc_max=math.log(maxc)
    exps=np.exp((np.log(c[pos])-logc_max)/temp)
    s=exps.sum()
    if s<=0: p[argmax]=1
    else:
       if top_frac>0:
          K=max(1,min(L,int(L*top_frac)))
          # idx_project? Since pos maybe, just top c all
          idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:] if K<L else np.arange(L)
          p[idx]=1.0/len(idx)
          # combine: temp softmax full
          p_temp=np.zeros(L); p_temp[pos]=exps/s
          p=0.6*p + 0.4*p_temp? Or 0.5. Ensure sum1.
       else:
          p[pos]=exps/s
    p /= p.sum()
    return p
```
For LP use top_frac=0.02; gradient custom top_frac=0.0 (pure) maybe. If pure T=0.2, p over logc; good. For L-BFGS pure with T maybe.

Implement `lbfgs_refine`:
```
def lbfgs_refine(a, budget_s, seed=0):
   n=a.size; target=math.sqrt(2*n); a=normalize_sequence(a,target)
   best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
   # initial x from log a, scaled
   b0=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); b0-=b0.mean(); m=max(abs(b0)); if m>4: b0*=4/m; b0-=mean
   x0=b0.copy()
   T_list=[0.8,0.4,0.2,0.1]
   maxiter = 400 if n<=512 else 200 if n<=1024 else 80 if n<=2048 else 40
   t0=now; deadline=t0+budget
   for T in T_list:
       if time > deadline-10: break
       global _LB_TEMP; _LB_TEMP=T
       try:
           res=minimize(_lbfgs_objective, x0, jac=_lbfgs_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(-6,6)]*n, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'gtol':1e-5,'maxls':10,'iprint':0})
           if res.x is not None:
              x0=np.asarray(res.x,float)
              b=x0-x0.mean(); m=max(abs(b)); if m>5: b*=5/m; b-=b.mean()
              a2=normalize_sequence(np.exp(b), target)
              sc=fast_score(a2)
              if sc<best_score: best=a2; best_score=sc
              x0=b if finite else x0
       except Exception:
           pass
   # after L-BFGS, maybe no custom here, return
   return best,best_score
```

The gradient of smooth objective `Phi = T logΣ exp(logc/T) - 2logS` is `Σ p_k ∂logc_k - 2a/S`. ∂logc_k = derivative as above. Good. We also add constant log(2n). This objective is invariant to scale? Let's check `cross_valid` for w in gradient. For `grad_logsum`: `q_i = 2 Σ_k p_k a_{k-i}/c_k`. We pass w_k = 2 p_k / c_k. Then q_i = cross_valid(a,w) = Σ_d=0^{n-1} a_d w_{d+i}. Here d = k-i, k = i+d. Yes. Good.

Potential off-by-one for diagonal double: We used w with 2 p/c. If c_k diagonal term a_i^2, derivative ∂logc/∂b_i includes 2a_i^2/c. In q, term for k=2i: w_{2i}=2p/c, q_i includes a_i*w/(?) actually q_i =Σ_d a_d w_{i+d}. For d=i, w_{2i}=2p/c, term=2 a_i^2 p/c. Good. For non-diagonal k=i+j (j≠i), derivative should 2 a_i a_j p/c. q_i has d=j term a_j w_{i+j}=2a_j p/c. Good. The other pair with i? The term i in sum c_k appears also? We already accounted by 2. Good.

**`weights_log_objective`**: If `temp` <=0, handle. Use `max( min?`. Code:
```
def weights_log_objective(c, temp=0.25, top_frac=0.0):
    L=int(c.size)
    p=np.zeros(L,float)
    if L==0: return p
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <=0 or not finite(temp) or temp<=0:
       p[int(np.argmax(c))] =1.0; return p
    tol = max(1e-12*cmax, 1e-300)
    pos = c > tol
    if not np.any(pos): p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; return p
    logc_max = math.log(cmax) # but cmax might not in pos? yes if max>tol
    vals = c[pos]
    exps = np.exp((np.log(vals)-logc_max)/float(temp))
    s = float(exps.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not finite(s):
       p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; return p
    p_tmp = np.zeros(L)
    p_tmp[pos] = exps/s
    if top_frac and top_frac >0:
       K = int(max(1, min(L, L*float(top_frac))))
       if K < L:
          idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       else:
          idx = np.arange(L)
       p_top = np.zeros(L); p_top[idx] = 1.0/float(len(idx))
       p = 0.7*p_top + 0.3*p_tmp
    else:
       p = p_tmp
    ts=p.sum()
    if ts>0: p/=ts
    else: p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
    return p
```
If cmax >0 but pos false because cmax<1e-300? then argmax. Good.

**`gradient_log_objective`**:
```
def gradient_log_objective(a,c):
    n=a.size
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<=0: return zeros
    p=weights_log? Should pass p.
    w=np.zeros(c.size)
    pos=c>0
    w[pos]=2.0*p[pos]/c[pos]
    q=cross_valid(a,w)
    grad = a*q
    grad = grad - 2.0*a/S
    grad -= np.mean(grad)
    if not finite: return zeros? 
    return grad
```
If c has zeros and p zero, pos fine. If p has NaN? p finite. OK.

```
def generate_starts(n, rng, parent_seq, random_count=12):
    n=int(n); starts=[]
    if parent_seq is not None:
       arr=np.asarray(parent_seq,float)
       if arr.size>0:
          add_start_to_list(starts, arr, n)
          # reversed maybe
          add_start_to_list(starts, arr[::-1], n)
    # uniform
    add_start_to_list(starts, np.ones(n), n)
    # two blocks
    for frac in (0.25,0.35,0.45,0.55,0.65,0.75):
       for ratio in (0.35,0.6,1.6,3.0):
          add_start_to_list(starts, make_two_block(n, frac, ratio), n)
    # exponentials
    if n>1:
        t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
        for alpha in (-4.0,-2.0,-1.0,1.0,2.0,4.0):
            add_start_to_list(starts, np.exp(alpha*(t-0.5)), n)
            add_start_to_list(starts, np.exp(alpha*t + 0.5*alpha*(1-t))? maybe just)
        for a1 in (-2.0,0.0,2.0):
           for a2 in (-2.0,0.0,2.0):
              z = a1*(t-0.5) + a2*(t-0.5)**2
              add_start_to_list(starts, np.exp(z), n)
    # random
    for i in range(random_count):
       if i%2==0: add make_block_sequence(n,rng, int(4+rng.integers(0,13)))
       else: add make_harmonic_sequence(n,rng)
    if not starts: add uniform
    starts.sort(key=_pair_score)
    return starts
```
This many deterministic: two-block 24, exp 6, quadratic 9, total ~40. For n=4096, 40 convs maybe 40*0.01=0.4s, fine. For 512 less. Could be okay. Random 12. Total 50.

Maybe reversed parent duplicate same score; add_start may include. Good.

**`upsample_sequence`**: For parent lengths, if old size 0 return ones. If old size 1, fill? `np.full(n, seq[0])`. If old size target n, return array. Else interp. Then normalize? ```
def upsample_sequence(seq, n):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,float)
    if seq.size==0: return np.ones(n)
    seq=np.where(finite,seq,0); seq=np.maximum(seq,0)
    n=int(n)
    if seq.size==n: return seq.copy()
    if seq.size==1: return np.full(n, seq[0])
    old=np.linspace(0,1,seq.size); new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.interp(new, old, seq)
    return out
```

def refine_level(n,budget_s,init_seq,seed,allow_lp=False):

    if budget_s <=0 or init_seq is None? fallback

    starts=generate_starts(n,rng,init_seq, random_count=16 if n<=1024 else 10)
    if not starts: starts=[(2.0, normalize_sequence(np.ones(n), target))]
    best_seq,best_score = starts[0][1].copy(), starts[0][0]
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s
    if budget_s > 600 and len(starts)>1:
       alloc1 = budget_s*0.65
       seq,sc=local_pipeline(starts[0][1], min(alloc1, deadline-now), seed, allow_lp)
       best=...
       rem=deadline-now
       if rem>120:
           # choose a different start (starts[1]) maybe if multiple
           seq2,sc2=local_pipeline(starts[1][1], max(10.0, rem-10.0), seed+1000, allow_lp)
           update
    else:
       seq,sc=local_pipeline(starts[0][1], max(0.0, budget_s-2.0), seed, allow_lp)
       update
    return best_seq,best_score

`add_candidate_pool`: Keep up to 16.

def add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, score):
    if seq is None: return
    sc=float(score)
    if not math.isfinite(sc): return
    pool.append((sc, np.asarray(seq,float).copy()))
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(pool)>16: pool.pop()
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=max(0.0,float(budget_s))
    print(f"budget {budget:.1f}s")
    # parent
    try: parent=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float)
    except: parent=None
    if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
       parent=np.where(np.isfinite(parent),parent,0); parent=np.maximum(parent,0)
    else: parent=None
    if budget < 20: return sanitize_for_return(parent if parent is not None else np.ones(512))
    search_buffer = max(30.0, min(120.0, 0.05*budget))
    search_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - search_buffer)
    # choose levels
    if budget < 150: levels=[512]
    elif budget < 700: levels=[512,1024]
    elif budget < 1600: levels=[512,1024,2048]
    else: levels=[512,1024,2048,4096]
    weights={512:1.0,1024:1.6,2048:2.8,4096:5.0}
    candidates=[]
    base_fallback = parent.copy() if parent is not None else np.ones(512)
    add_candidate_pool(candidates, base_fallback, fast_score_array(base_fallback))
    global_best_seq=base_fallback.copy(); global_best_score=...
    for idx,L in enumerate(levels):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= search_deadline - 30: break
       remaining = max(0.0, search_deadline - now)
       future_w = sum(weights[lev] for lev in levels[idx:])
       frac = weights[L] / future_w
       level_budget = max(0.0, remaining * frac)
       if level_budget < 15: break
       init_seq = global_best_seq if global_best_seq is not None else np.ones(L)
       allow_lp = L <= 760
       try:

       except Exception as e:

    if exact_best_seq is None: use fallback
    final = sanitize_for_return(exact_best_seq)
    print final
    return final

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The implementation combines several methods:
- FFT-based objective evaluation.
- A few deterministic and random smooth starting shapes, plus the inherited
  parent construction if available.
- A linear-programming descent step (fixed-a feasible-direction LP) at smaller
  lengths to get out of rough basins.
- A smooth log-domain L-BFGS-B polish. The objective is written in terms of
  log-coefficients and a soft max of log convolution coefficients.
- A final multiplicative log-gradient polish with restarts, using active
  weightings biased toward dominant convolution coefficients.
- Coarse-to-fine length search, followed by exact-evaluation validation of the
  best candidates before returning.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize

# Global temperature used by the top-level L-BFGS objective/gradient functions.
# It is updated by lbfgs_refine() before each L-BFGS run.
_LB_TEMP = 0.25


def _pair_score(pair):
    return float(pair[0])


def fast_convolution(a):
    """FFT-based linear convolution a * a, length 2n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N, n=N)[:L]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def fast_score_array(a):
    """Fast scale-invariant analogue of evaluate_sequence(), without clipping."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")

    c = fast_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")

    maxc = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")

    return float(2.0 * n * maxc / (s * s))


def normalize_sequence(a, target_sum):
    """Normalize a non-negative sequence to the desired sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a

    if not math.isfinite(target_sum) or target_sum <= 0.0:
        target_sum = 1.0

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        a.fill(float(target_sum) / float(a.size))
        return a

    a *= float(target_sum) / s
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    return a


def positive_floor(n, target_sum):
    """A tiny positive floor to keep log-space updates well behaved."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e-12
    return max(1e-12, 1e-8 * (float(target_sum) / float(n)))


def upsample_sequence(seq, n):
    """Resample to length n.  No normalization is performed."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if seq.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.maximum(seq, 0.0)

    if seq.size == n:
        return seq.copy()

    if seq.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(seq[0]), dtype=np.float64)

    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(xnew, xold, seq).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def add_start_to_list(starts, a, n):
    """Normalize and score a candidate start, appending it if valid."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        n = int(n)
        if a.size != n:
            a = upsample_sequence(a, n)
        target = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
        a = normalize_sequence(a, target)
        sc = fast_score_array(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            starts.append((float(sc), a))
    except Exception:
        pass


def make_two_block(n, frac, ratio):
    """Two-block positive sequence with left block height ratio*r."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    m = int(round(float(frac) * n))
    m = max(1, min(n - 1, m))

    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = float(ratio)
    return a


def make_block_sequence(n, rng, num_blocks=8):
    """Random positive piecewise-constant sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 2:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    num_blocks = max(2, min(int(num_blocks), n // 2))
    edges = np.round(np.linspace(0.0, n, num_blocks + 1)).astype(int)
    edges[0] = 0
    edges[-1] = n
    lengths = np.diff(edges)

    if np.any(lengths <= 0):
        edges = np.linspace(0.0, n, num_blocks + 1).astype(int)
        edges[0] = 0
        edges[-1] = n
        lengths = np.diff(edges)

    vals = rng.uniform(0.1, 3.0, num_blocks)
    if rng.random() < 0.2:
        k = int(rng.integers(0, num_blocks))
        vals[k] = 0.05 + 0.1 * rng.random()

    return np.repeat(vals, lengths).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def make_harmonic_sequence(n, rng):
    """Positive low-frequency smooth random shape."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, 7):
        amp = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) / float(m)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + ph)

    z -= float(z.max())
    return np.exp(z).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def make_parametric_sequence(n, alpha, beta):
    """Positive sequence alpha*(x-1/2) + beta*(x-1/2)^2 in log space."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = t - 0.5
    z = float(alpha) * u + float(beta) * (u * u)
    z -= float(z.max())
    return np.exp(z).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def generate_starts(n, rng, parent_seq, random_count=12):
    """Build a sorted list of promising starts at length n."""
    n = int(n)
    starts = []

    if parent_seq is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(parent_seq, dtype=np.float64)
            if p.size > 0:
                add_start_to_list(starts, p, n)
                add_start_to_list(starts, p[::-1], n)
        except Exception:
            pass

    add_start_to_list(starts, np.ones(n), n)

    # Asymmetric two-block shapes.
    for frac in (0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70, 0.80):
        for ratio in (0.30, 0.55, 1.70, 3.20):
            add_start_to_list(starts, make_two_block(n, frac, ratio), n)

    # Log-linear and log-quadratic positive shapes.
    if n > 1:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        for alpha in (-4.0, -2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
            add_start_to_list(starts, np.exp(alpha * (t - 0.5)), n)

        for alpha in (-2.0, 0.0, 2.0):
            for beta in (-2.0, 0.0, 2.0):
                add_start_to_list(starts, make_parametric_sequence(n, alpha, beta), n)

    for i in range(max(0, int(random_count))):
        if i % 2 == 0:
            nb = int(rng.integers(4, 17))
            add_start_to_list(starts, make_block_sequence(n, rng, nb), n)
        else:
            add_start_to_list(starts, make_harmonic_sequence(n, rng), n)

    if not starts:
        target = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
        starts.append((2.0, normalize_sequence(np.ones(n), target)))

    starts.sort(key=_pair_score)
    return starts


def smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.35):
    """Smooth multiplicative perturbation for restarts."""
    target = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(a.size))
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    n = a.size
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    if n > 2:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 5):
            amp = float(strength) / (0.5 + 0.5 * float(m))
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + ph)
        a *= np.exp(z)

    if n > 4 and rng.random() < 0.45:
        low = max(1, n // 200)
        high = max(low + 1, n // 10 + 1)
        width = int(rng.integers(low, min(high, n)))
        stop = max(1, n - width + 1)
        if stop > 0:
            start = int(rng.integers(0, stop))
            stop_end = min(n, start + width)
            a[start:stop_end] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(strength), stop_end - start))

    floor = positive_floor(n, target)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    return normalize_sequence(a, target)


def weights_log_objective(c, temp=0.25, top_frac=0.0):
    """
    Weights over convolution coefficients for the smooth log-max objective.

    Uses exp((log c - max log c)/temp), optionally mixed with a uniform
    top-fraction active set.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 0:
        return p

    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        p[0] = 1.0
        return p

    if not math.isfinite(temp) or temp <= 0.0:
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p

    tol = max(1e-12 * cmax, 1e-300)
    pos = c > tol
    if not np.any(pos):
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p

    logc_max = math.log(cmax)
    vals = c[pos]
    exps = np.exp((np.log(vals) - logc_max) / float(temp))
    s = float(exps.sum())

    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p

    p_soft = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    p_soft[pos] = exps / s

    if top_frac is not None and top_frac > 0.0:
        K = int(max(1, min(L, L * float(top_frac))))
        if K < L:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(L)

        p_top = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p_top[idx] = 1.0 / float(len(idx))
        p = 0.7 * p_top + 0.3 * p_soft
    else:
        p = p_soft

    ps = float(p.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0.0:
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p /= ps

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    ps2 = float(p.sum())
    if ps2 <= 0.0:
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p /= ps2

    return p


def cross_valid(a, w):
    """
    Valid cross-correlation needed for the A^T p type products.

    Returns q[j] = sum_{d=0}^{n-1} a[d] * w[d+j], length n.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if w.size != 2 * n - 1:
        raise ValueError("len(w) must be 2*n-1")

    conv_len = 3 * n - 2
    N = 1 << max(0, conv_len - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, N, n=N)[:conv_len]

    # For q[j], see derivation in the code comments above.
    return conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1].copy()


def gradient_log_objective(a, c, p):
    """
    Gradient of the smooth log-objective
        T log sum_k exp(log(c_k)/T) - 2 log(sum a)
    with weights p corresponding to the soft max of log(c).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = c > 1e-300
    w[pos] = 2.0 * p[pos] / c[pos]
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)

    q = cross_valid(a, w)
    grad = a * q
    grad = grad - 2.0 * a / s
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    grad -= float(grad.mean())
    return grad


def make_lp_matrix(a):
    """Dense matrix A such that A @ g == np.convolve(a, g)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(n)

    for d in range(n):
        val = float(a[d])
        if val != 0.0 and math.isfinite(val):
            A[d + idx, idx] = val
    return A


def solve_lp_direction(a, M, bias_vec, target_sum, bias=0.5, time_limit=20.0):
    """
    Solve the LP in the inherited method:
        maximize  (-1 + bias*d/N_d) . g
        subject to   g >= 0,  convolve(a, g) <= M.

    The result is rescaled to sum = target_sum and projected back inside the
    convolution bound if needed.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None

    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if bias_vec is not None and np.asarray(bias_vec).size == n:
        d = np.asarray(bias_vec, dtype=np.float64)
        d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
        dmax = float(d.max())
        if math.isfinite(dmax) and dmax > 1e-14:
            d_use = np.maximum(d, 0.0) / dmax
            b = min(0.9, max(0.0, float(bias)))
            c_obj += b * d_use

    A = make_lp_matrix(a)
    b_ub = float(M) * np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(time_limit), "maxiter": 20000},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None:
        return None

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)

    s = float(x.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None

    g = x * (float(target_sum) / s)
    fg = fast_convolution(a, g) if False else np.convolve(a, g)  # exact, only small LP lengths
    maxfg = float(fg.max()) if fg.size else 0.0
    if math.isfinite(maxfg) and maxfg > float(M):
        if maxfg > 1e-300:
            g *= float(M) / maxfg
        else:
            g[:] = 0.0

    s2 = float(g.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 1e-12:
        return None

    g /= s2
    g *= float(target_sum)
    return np.maximum(g, 0.0)


def lp_refine(a, budget_s, seed=0):
    """Run several LP-direction line-search steps."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0 or budget_s <= 15.0:
        sc = fast_score_array(a)
        return a, sc

    target = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score_array(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    it = 0

    t_values = (
        0.01, 0.02, 0.035, 0.06, 0.10, 0.16,
        0.25, 0.38, 0.55, 0.75, 0.92, 1.00
    )

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0 and it < 120:
        c = fast_convolution(a)
        score = float(c.max())  # target normalization means fast_score == max(c)
        sc = fast_score_array(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = sc

        M = score * (1.0 + 1e-8) + 1e-12
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        p = weights_log_objective(c, temp=0.25, top_frac=0.01)
        d = cross_valid(a, p)
        d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)

        bias = min(0.85, 0.35 + 0.05 * float(it))
        g = solve_lp_direction(a, M, d, target, bias=bias, time_limit=18.0)
        if g is None:
            break

        local_best = a.copy()
        local_best_score = score

        for tt in t_values:
            h = (1.0 - tt) * a + tt * g
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            h = normalize_sequence(h, target)
            hh = fast_convolution(h)
            scand = float(hh.max()) if hh.size else float("inf")
            sc_and = fast_score_array(h)
            if math.isfinite(sc_and):
                scand = min(scand, sc_and)

            if math.isfinite(scand) and scand < local_best_score - 1e-12:
                local_best = h.copy()
                local_best_score = scand

        if math.isfinite(local_best_score) and local_best_score < score - 1e-12:
            a = local_best
            it += 1
        else:
            break

    if math.isfinite(fast_score_array(best)):
        return best, fast_score_array(best)
    return a, fast_score_array(a)


def gradient_log_refine(a, budget_s, seed=0):
    """Multiplicative log-space descent for the log-ratio objective."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0 or budget_s <= 5.0:
        return a, fast_score_array(a)

    target = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    floor = positive_floor(n, target)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score_array(a)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    lr = 0.12
    beta = 0.55
    accept_tol = 0.035
    noise = 0.012
    temp = 0.35
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
            a = best.copy()
            mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        c = fast_convolution(a)
        score = fast_score_array(a)
        if not math.isfinite(score):
            a = best.copy()
            score = best_score
            stagnant += 100
            if stagnant > 250:
                break
            continue

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = score

        p = weights_log_objective(c, temp=temp, top_frac=0.0)
        grad = gradient_log_objective(a, c, p)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if not math.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-14:
            stagnant += 10
            if stagnant > 250:
                break
            continue

        grad = grad / gmax
        mom = beta * mom + (1.0 - beta) * grad
        delta = -lr * mom
        if noise > 1e-7:
            delta = delta + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
        delta = np.clip(delta, -3.0, 3.0)

        cand = a * np.exp(delta)
        cand = np.maximum(cand, floor)
        cand = normalize_sequence(cand, target)
        cand_score = fast_score_array(cand)

        if not math.isfinite(cand_score):
            lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.75)
            mom *= 0.5
            stagnant += 1
            continue

        if cand_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand.copy()
            best_score = cand_score

        old_score = score
        if cand_score <= old_score * (1.0 + accept_tol) + 1e-14:
            a = cand
            score = cand_score
            mom *= 0.85

            if cand_score < old_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                lr = min(0.60, lr * 1.04)
                accept_tol = max(0.002, accept_tol * 0.998)
                noise = max(0.001, noise * 0.998)
                temp = max(0.06, temp * 0.999)
                stagnant = 0
            else:
                lr = max(0.04, lr * 0.97)
                stagnant += 1
        else:
            lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.78)
            mom *= 0.5
            accept_tol = min(0.16, accept_tol * 1.06)
            noise = min(0.09, noise * 1.08)
            temp = min(1.0, temp * 1.03)
            stagnant += 1

        if stagnant > 550:
            new_a = smooth_perturb(best, rng, 0.38)
            new_score = fast_score_array(new_a)
            if math.isfinite(new_score) and new_score <= best_score * 1.08:
                a = new_a
                score = new_score
            else:
                a = best.copy()
                score = best_score
            lr = 0.25
            accept_tol = 0.10
            noise = 0.045
            temp = 0.50
            mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            stagnant = 0

        it += 1

    if math.isfinite(fast_score_array(best)):
        return best, fast_score_array(best)
    return a, fast_score_array(a)


def _lbfgs_make_b(x):
    """Project x to zero mean, removing scale invariance."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    return x - float(x.mean())


def _lbfgs_objective(x):
    """Smooth log-domain objective used by L-BFGS-B."""
    b = _lbfgs_make_b(x)
    n = b.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0

    a = np.exp(b)
    c = fast_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0

    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e100

    logc_max = math.log(cmax)
    pos = c > 1e-300
    if not np.any(pos):
        return 1e100

    T = float(_LB_TEMP)
    exps = np.exp((np.log(c[pos]) - logc_max) / T)
    s = float(exps.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return 1e100

    logsum = logc_max + T * math.log(s)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return 1e100

    return float(logsum - 2.0 * math.log(S) + math.log(2.0 * n))


def _lbfgs_gradient(x):
    """Gradient of _lbfgs_objective."""
    b = _lbfgs_make_b(x)
    n = b.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.exp(b)
    c = fast_convolution(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if c.size == 0 or not math.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    logc_max = math.log(cmax)
    pos = c > 1e-300
    if not np.any(pos):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    T = float(_LB_TEMP)
    exps = np.exp((np.log(c[pos]) - logc_max) / T)
    s = float(exps.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[pos] = exps / s

    return gradient_log_objective(a, c, p)


def lbfgs_refine(a, budget_s, seed=0):
    """Polish using several L-BFGS-B runs with decreasing soft-max temperature."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0 or budget_s <= 20.0:
        return a, fast_score_array(a)

    target = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)
    floor = positive_floor(n, target)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score_array(a)

    b0 = np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
    b0 = b0 - float(b0.mean())
    m0 = float(np.max(np.abs(b0))) if b0.size else 1.0
    if math.isfinite(m0) and m0 > 5.0:
        b0 *= 5.0 / m0
    b0 -= float(b0.mean())
    x0 = b0.copy()

    if budget_s > 1200:
        T_list = (1.1, 0.65, 0.38, 0.22, 0.13)
    elif budget_s > 500:
        T_list = (1.0, 0.55, 0.30, 0.17)
    elif budget_s > 200:
        T_list = (0.9, 0.45, 0.25)
    else:
        T_list = (0.7, 0.35)

    if n <= 512:
        base_iter = 320
    elif n <= 1024:
        base_iter = 170
    elif n <= 2048:
        base_iter = 80
    else:
        base_iter = 42

    if budget_s < 250:
        base_iter = max(30, base_iter // 2)

    bounds = [(-6.0, 6.0)] * n

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    for T in T_list:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
            break

        global _LB_TEMP
        _LB_TEMP = float(T)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lbfgs_objective,
                x0,
                jac=_lbfgs_gradient,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(base_iter),
                    "gtol": 1e-5,
                    "maxls": 8 if n > 1500 else 12,
                    "iprint": 0,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and res.x is not None:
            xres = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            xres = np.where(np.isfinite(xres), xres, 0.0)
            b = xres - float(xres.mean())

            mb = float(np.max(np.abs(b))) if b.size else 0.0
            if math.isfinite(mb) and mb > 4.5:
                b *= 4.5 / mb
            b -= float(b.mean())

            a2 = np.exp(b)
            a2 = np.where(np.isfinite(a2), a2, 0.0)
            a2 = np.maximum(a2, floor)
            a2 = normalize_sequence(a2, target)
            sc = fast_score_array(a2)

            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = a2.copy()
                best_score = sc
                x0 = b.copy()
            else:
                # Keep a safe starting point for the next temperature.
                x0 = b.copy()

    return best, best_score


def update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, score):
    """Return improved (sequence, score) pair if possible."""
    if seq is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    try:
        sc = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return best_seq, best_score

    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return best_seq, best_score

    if best_seq is None or sc < float(best_score) - 1e-12:
        return np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy(), sc
    return best_seq, best_score


def local_pipeline(a, budget_s, seed=0, allow_lp=False):
    """LP + L-BFGS + gradient polish on one length and start."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    target = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    floor = positive_floor(n, target)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)

    best_seq = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score_array(a)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    if allow_lp and n <= 760:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 60.0:
            lp_b = min(0.35 * float(budget_s), 300.0, rem - 30.0)
            if lp_b > 20.0:
                try:
                    seq, sc = lp_refine(a, lp_b, int(seed))
                except Exception:
                    seq, sc = None, float("inf")
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc)
                if seq is not None and math.isfinite(sc):
                    a = seq.copy()
                    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
                    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        max_lb = 380.0 if n <= 1024 else 210.0
        lb_b = min(0.35 * rem, max_lb, rem - 30.0)
        if lb_b > 20.0:
            try:
                seq, sc = lbfgs_refine(a, lb_b, int(seed) + 101)
            except Exception:
                seq, sc = None, float("inf")
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc)
            if seq is not None and math.isfinite(sc):
                a = seq.copy()
                a = np.maximum(a, floor)
                a = normalize_sequence(a, target)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 10.0:
        try:
            seq, sc = gradient_log_refine(a, max(5.0, rem - 3.0), int(seed) + 211)
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = None, float("inf")
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc)

    if best_seq is None or not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n), target)
        best_score = fast_score_array(best_seq)

    return best_seq, best_score


def refine_level(n, budget_s, init_seq, seed=0, allow_lp=False):
    """Optimize one length from a pool of starts and possible explores."""
    n = int(n)
    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return (init_seq if init_seq is not None else np.ones(n)), float("inf")

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Use the inherited parent/init as one start plus diverse shape starts.
    starts = generate_starts(
        n,
        rng,
        init_seq,
        random_count=12 if n > 2000 else 16
    )

    best_seq, best_score = starts[0][1].copy(), starts[0][0]
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s

    if budget_s > 700.0 and len(starts) > 1:
        alloc1 = budget_s * 0.65
        try:
            seq, sc = local_pipeline(
                starts[0][1],
                max(5.0, min(alloc1, deadline - time.monotonic())),
                int(seed),
                allow_lp=allow_lp,
            )
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = None, float("inf")
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 160.0:
            # Explore a different good start to avoid the primary basin.
            try:
                seq2, sc2 = local_pipeline(
                    starts[1][1],
                    max(5.0, rem - 8.0),
                    int(seed) + 2000,
                    allow_lp=allow_lp,
                )
            except Exception:
                seq2, sc2 = None, float("inf")
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq2, sc2)
    else:
        try:
            seq, sc = local_pipeline(
                starts[0][1],
                max(5.0, budget_s - 3.0),
                int(seed),
                allow_lp=allow_lp,
            )
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = None, float("inf")
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc)

    return best_seq, best_score


def add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, score):
    """Maintain a sorted small pool of candidate sequences."""
    if seq is None:
        return
    try:
        sc = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return

    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return

    pool.append((sc, np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(pool) > 16:
        pool.pop()


def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    """Return a valid list of non-negative finite floats, max <= 1000."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return [1.0] * 512

    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.maximum(seq, 0.0)

    s = float(seq.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return [1.0] * 512

    mx = float(seq.max())
    if math.isfinite(mx) and mx > 1000.0:
        scale = 1000.0 / mx
        seq = seq * scale
        s2 = float(seq.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s2) or s2 < 0.01:
            return [1.0] * 512

    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(v) for v in seq]


def _load_parent():
    """Load the inherited parent construction if available."""
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None

    if p is None or p.size == 0:
        return None

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    if float(p.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return None
    return p


def _fallback_sequence(parent):
    if parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.size > 0 and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
            return p
    return np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search entry point.

    Returns a list of non-negative heights minimizing the evaluation function.
    The sequence is internally normalized to sum=sqrt(2n), then scaled before
    return if necessary to avoid the evaluator's 1000 clip.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    print(f"propose_candidate: budget={budget:.1f}s", flush=True)

    parent = _load_parent()
    base_seq = _fallback_sequence(parent)
    base_score = fast_score_array(base_seq)
    if not math.isfinite(base_score):
        base_seq = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        base_score = 2.0

    if budget < 30.0:
        return sanitize_for_return(base_seq)

    # Leave a buffer for exact validation and returning.
    buffer = max(45.0, min(110.0, 0.045 * budget))
    search_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - buffer)
    final_deadline = t0 + max(buffer + 1.0, budget - 5.0)

    if budget < 220.0:
        levels = [512]
    elif budget < 900.0:
        levels = [512, 1024]
    elif budget < 1800.0:
        levels = [512, 1024, 2048]
    else:
        levels = [512, 1024, 2048, 4096]

    weights = {512: 1.0, 1024: 1.6, 2048: 2.8, 4096: 5.0}

    pool = []
    add_candidate_pool(pool, base_seq, base_score)

    global_best_seq = base_seq.copy()
    global_best_score = base_score

    for idx, L in enumerate(levels):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= search_deadline - 25.0:
            break

        remaining = max(0.0, search_deadline - now)
        future_w = sum(weights[x] for x in levels[idx:])
        level_budget = remaining * (weights[L] / max(1e-12, future_w))

        if level_budget < 18.0:
            break

        init_seq = global_best_seq if global_best_seq is not None else np.ones(L)
        allow_lp = L <= 760

        try:
            seq, sc = refine_level(
                L,
                level_budget,
                init_seq,
                int(seed) + 3000 + idx * 97,
                allow_lp=allow_lp,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"Level {L} failed: {exc}", flush=True)
            seq, sc = None, float("inf")

        if seq is not None:
            seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
            add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, sc)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_best_score - 1e-12:
                global_best_score = sc
                global_best_seq = seq.copy()

        print(
            f"level n={L}: best_fast={global_best_score:.10f} "
            f"remaining={max(0.0, search_deadline - time.monotonic()):.1f}s",
            flush=True
        )

    # Exact validation of the most promising candidates.
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    exact_best_seq = global_best_seq
    exact_best_score = global_best_score

    for i, (sc, seq) in enumerate(pool[:8]):
        if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 2.0:
            break

        lst = sanitize_for_return(seq)
        if not lst:
            continue

        try:
            ex = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            ex = float("inf")

        if math.isfinite(ex) and ex < exact_best_score - 1e-12:
            exact_best_score = ex
            exact_best_seq = np.asarray(lst, dtype=np.float64)

        if i < 3:
            print(
                f"exact candidate {i}: fast={sc:.10f} exact={ex:.10f}",
                flush=True
            )

    if exact_best_seq is None or not math.isfinite(exact_best_score):
        exact_best_seq = base_seq

    final_list = sanitize_for_return(exact_best_seq)
    print(f"final returned length={len(final_list)}", flush=True)
    return final_list
```
